- **brainstorming-ux-features**: Use when asked to brainstorm UX features, prioritize evidence-backed product ideas, or convert the strongest idea into an agent-parsable feature.json implementation contract. Trigger on brainstorm UX features, find high-impact UX enhancements, create a feature proposal, prepare an implementation-ready feature spec. NOT for generic UI visual styling; use frontend-design instead, and NOT for a fully scoped multi-file implementation plan; use the improve skill instead. Requires citing friction signals, user goals, or workflow evidence from the repository before proposing an idea. ([JackSmack1971/CCSIF](https://github.com/JackSmack1971/CCSIF/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brainstorming-ux-features))
- **brainstorming-values-archaeology**: Facilitate a Values Archaeology session that excavates the unstated values and basic assumptions driving a team's or stakeholder's decisions. Invoke when stakeholder conflict, charter disagreements, or recurring scope disputes appear to stem from misaligned underlying priorities rather than factual differences. ([Agentic-Engineering-Agency/agentic-pm-kit](https://github.com/Agentic-Engineering-Agency/agentic-pm-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/pm-kit/brainstorming-values-archaeology) · ★2)
